{"type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01Y5tS3DAVJFjujtAVq5dpWj","type":"tool_result","content":"     1→```python\n     2→\"\"\"\n     3→FlockDesk – Social Workspace Orchestrator\n     4→-----------------------------------------\n     5→\n     6→core.ipc.event_bus\n     7→~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~\n     8→\n     9→A lightweight, **process-safe** event-bus implementation that acts as the glue\n    10→between the independent micro-front-ends that compose the FlockDesk desktop\n    11→suite.  The implementation purposefully avoids external dependencies and\n    12→relies solely on the standard library (``asyncio``/``multiprocessing``) so that\n    13→plugins do not need to vendor additional wheels.\n    14→\n    15→Design goals\n    16→============\n    17→\n    18→1. Publish / subscribe with topic-based routing and Unix‐shell wild-card\n    19→   matching (``fnmatch``) so that a handler can listen to groups\n    20→   (e.g. ``\"presence.*\"``).\n    21→\n    22→2. Support both synchronous **and** asynchronous handlers without forcing plugin\n    23→   authors to learn ``asyncio``.\n    24→\n    25→3. Provide a *request / reply* pattern with correlation-IDs and automatic\n    26→   timeout handling—essential for client–server style interactions.\n    27→\n    28→4. Remain safe across processes.  A *broker* process (spawned lazily and managed\n    29→   via :pymod:`multiprocessing.managers`) forwards events between producer and\n    30→   consumer processes transparently.\n    31→\n    32→5. Offer graceful shutdown semantics so that the application can quit quickly\n    33→   even under heavy traffic.\n    34→\n    35→The broker architecture may feel heavyweight for local desktop IPC, but it\n    36→guarantees that crashing micro-front-ends cannot bring down the other windows.\n    37→\"\"\"\n    38→\n    39→from __future__ import annotations\n    40→\n    41→import asyncio\n    42→import contextlib\n    43→import fnmatch\n    44→import inspect\n    45→import logging\n    46→import queue\n    47→import threading\n    48→import time\n    49→import uuid\n    50→from dataclasses import dataclass, field\n    51→from multiprocessing import Event as MpEvent\n    52→from multiprocessing import Process, Queue\n    53→from multiprocessing.managers import BaseManager\n    54→from types import TracebackType\n    55→from typing import (\n    56→    Any,\n    57→    Awaitable,\n    58→    Callable,\n    59→    Dict,\n    60→    List,\n    61→    Mapping,\n    62→    MutableMapping,\n    63→    Optional,\n    64→    Tuple,\n    65→    Type,\n    66→    Union,\n    67→)\n    68→\n    69→# -----------------------------------------------------------------------------\n    70→# Logging\n    71→# -----------------------------------------------------------------------------\n    72→_log = logging.getLogger(\"flockdesk.ipc.event_bus\")\n    73→_log.addHandler(logging.NullHandler())\n    74→\n    75→# -----------------------------------------------------------------------------\n    76→# Exceptions\n    77→# -----------------------------------------------------------------------------\n    78→\n    79→\n    80→class EventBusError(RuntimeError):\n    81→    \"\"\"Base-class for all event-bus exceptions.\"\"\"\n    82→\n    83→\n    84→class HandlerTimeout(EventBusError):\n    85→    \"\"\"Raised by ``request`` when no reply arrived in time.\"\"\"\n    86→\n    87→\n    88→# -----------------------------------------------------------------------------\n    89→# Event primitives\n    90→# -----------------------------------------------------------------------------\n    91→\n    92→\n    93→@dataclass(slots=True, frozen=True)\n    94→class Event:\n    95→    \"\"\"\n    96→    A single immutable message traveling through the *in-memory* event bus.\n    97→\n    98→    Attributes\n    99→    ----------\n   100→    topic:\n   101→        Hierarchical, ``dot``-separated topic name\n   102→        (e.g. ``\"presence.updated\"``).\n   103→    payload:\n   104→        Opaque data expected by subscribers.  Must be *pickleable* because the\n   105→        broker relies on :pymod:`multiprocessing.Queue`.\n   106→    headers:\n   107→        Optional free-form metadata such as authentication tokens or tracing\n   108→        information.  Header values must again be pickleable.\n   109→    event_id:\n   110→        Unique identifier for tracing/debugging purposes.\n   111→    timestamp:\n   112→        Epoch seconds (float) at creation time.\n   113→    origin_pid:\n   114→        The operating-system process identifier of the sender.  Helpful when the\n   115→        same user runs multiple instances of FlockDesk on the same machine.\n   116→    \"\"\"\n   117→\n   118→    topic: str\n   119→    payload: Any\n   120→    headers: Mapping[str, Any] = field(default_factory=dict)\n   121→    event_id: str = field(default_factory=lambda: uuid.uuid4().hex, init=False)\n   122→    timestamp: float = field(default_factory=time.time, init=False)\n   123→    origin_pid: int = field(default_factory=lambda: threading.get_native_id(), init=False)\n   124→\n   125→    def __post_init__(self) -> None:  # noqa: D401\n   126→        \"\"\"Validate that the topic is non-empty.\"\"\"\n   127→        if not self.topic or not isinstance(self.topic, str):\n   128→            raise ValueError(\"Event.topic must be a non-empty string\")\n   129→\n   130→\n   131→# -----------------------------------------------------------------------------\n   132→# Subscription Handle\n   133→# -----------------------------------------------------------------------------\n   134→\n   135→\n   136→class _Subscription:\n   137→    \"\"\"\n   138→    Lightweight handle that allows clients to stop listening without keeping\n   139→    implementation details around.  Instances can also be used as *async*\n   140→    context-managers.\n   141→\n   142→    NOTE: Users are **not** expected to instantiate this class directly.\n   143→    \"\"\"\n   144→\n   145→    __slots__ = (\"_topic\", \"_handler\", \"_bus\", \"_active\")\n   146→\n   147→    def __init__(\n   148→        self,\n   149→        topic: str,\n   150→        handler: \"Handler\",\n   151→        bus: \"EventBus\",\n   152→    ) -> None:\n   153→        self._topic = topic\n   154→        self._handler = handler\n   155→        self._bus = bus\n   156→        self._active = True\n   157→\n   158→    async def __aenter__(self) -> \"_Subscription\":\n   159→        return self\n   160→\n   161→    async def __aexit__(\n   162→        self,\n   163→        exc_type: Optional[Type[BaseException]],\n   164→        exc: Optional[BaseException],\n   165→        tb: Optional[TracebackType],\n   166→    ) -> None:\n   167→        self.unsubscribe()\n   168→\n   169→    def unsubscribe(self) -> None:\n   170→        \"\"\"Remove the handler from the bus.\"\"\"\n   171→        if self._active:\n   172→            self._bus._remove_handler(self._topic, self._handler)  # noqa: SLF001\n   173→            self._active = False\n   174→\n   175→\n   176→# -----------------------------------------------------------------------------\n   177→# Type aliases\n   178→# -----------------------------------------------------------------------------\n   179→\n   180→SyncHandler = Callable[[Event], None]\n   181→AsyncHandler = Callable[[Event], Awaitable[None]]\n   182→Handler = Union[SyncHandler, AsyncHandler]\n   183→\n   184→# Internal maps\n   185→_HandlerMap = Dict[str, List[Handler]]\n   186→\n   187→\n   188→# -----------------------------------------------------------------------------\n   189→# Broker (separate process)\n   190→# -----------------------------------------------------------------------------\n   191→\n   192→# Queues are *multiprocessing* queues, therefore pickle based and safe between\n   193→# forked/spawned processes.\n   194→_InboundQueue = Queue  # alias for readability\n   195→_OutboundQueue = Queue\n   196→\n   197→\n   198→class _Broker(Process):\n   199→    \"\"\"\n   200→    Spin-up a separate process that forwards a single *inbound* queue to many\n   201→    *outbound* queues (fan-out).\n   202→\n   203→    Each process that calls ``EventBus.connect()`` will create its own outbound\n   204→    queue, while the producing bus writes to a single inbound queue.\n   205→\n   206→    Diagram::\n   207→\n   208→        ┌────────────────────┐\n   209→        │  client process A  │──┐\n   210→        └────────────────────┘  │  ┌────────────┐\n   211→                                ├─▶│            │\n   212→        ┌────────────────────┐  │  │            │\n   213→        │  client process B  │──┼─▶│   BROKER   │──▶ Inbound\n   214→        └────────────────────┘  │  │            │    Queue\n   215→                                └─▶│            │\n   216→                                   └────────────┘\n   217→\n   218→    \"\"\"\n   219→\n   220→    def __init__(self, inbound: _InboundQueue, shutdown_evt: MpEvent):\n   221→        super().__init__(name=\"FlockDeskEventBroker\", daemon=True)\n   222→        self._inbound: _InboundQueue = inbound\n   223→        self._shutdown_evt: MpEvent = shutdown_evt\n   224→        self._outbounds: List[_OutboundQueue] = []\n   225→\n   226→    # ------------------------------------------------------------------#\n   227→    # Public API for other processes (via SyncManager)\n   228→    # ------------------------------------------------------------------#\n   229→    def register_consumer(self) -> _OutboundQueue:\n   230→        \"\"\"\n   231→        Called by a client process to obtain a dedicated outbound queue.\n   232→        \"\"\"\n   233→        q: _OutboundQueue = Queue()\n   234→        self._outbounds.append(q)\n   235→        _log.debug(\"Broker registered new consumer queue (total=%d)\", len(self._outbounds))\n   236→        return q\n   237→\n   238→    # ------------------------------------------------------------------#\n   239→    # Main loop\n   240→    # ------------------------------------------------------------------#\n   241→    def run(self) -> None:  # noqa: D401\n   242→        _log.info(\"Event-bus broker running (PID=%d)\", self.pid)\n   243→        try:\n   244→            while not self._shutdown_evt.is_set():\n   245→                try:\n   246→                    evt: Event = self._inbound.get(timeout=0.1)\n   247→                except queue.Empty:\n   248→                    continue\n   249→\n   250→                # Broadcast\n   251→                for q in self._outbounds:\n   252→                    try:\n   253→                        q.put_nowait(evt)\n   254→                    except Exception:  # noqa: BLE001\n   255→                        _log.exception(\"Failed to forward event to queue %s\", q)\n   256→        finally:\n   257→            _log.info(\"Event-bus broker shutting down (PID=%d)\", self.pid)\n   258→\n   259→\n   260→# -----------------------------------------------------------------------------\n   261→# Multiprocessing manager that exposes broker methods\n   262→# -----------------------------------------------------------------------------\n   263→\n   264→\n   265→class _BusManager(BaseManager):\n   266→    pass\n   267→\n   268→\n   269→# have to register after class definition\n   270→_BusManager.register(\"register_consumer\", callable=None)  # type: ignore[arg-type]\n   271→\n   272→\n   273→# -----------------------------------------------------------------------------\n   274→# EventBus (public)\n   275→# -----------------------------------------------------------------------------\n   276→class EventBus:\n   277→    \"\"\"\n   278→    Public entry point used by every micro-front-end.\n   279→\n   280→    Processes talk to a shared broker, threads inside the same process share\n   281→    exactly one :class:`EventBus` instance (thread-safe singleton).\n   282→    \"\"\"\n   283→\n   284→    _singleton: \"EventBus\" | None = None\n   285→    _singleton_lock = threading.Lock()\n   286→\n   287→    # ------------------------------------------------------------------#\n   288→    # Construction helpers\n   289→    # ------------------------------------------------------------------#\n   290→    @classmethod\n   291→    def connect(cls) -> \"EventBus\":\n   292→        \"\"\"\n   293→        Return a *singleton* instance of :class:`EventBus` for the current\n   294→        process.  Lazily starts the broker **once** per workstation.\n   295→        \"\"\"\n   296→        with cls._singleton_lock:\n   297→            if cls._singleton is None:\n   298→                cls._singleton = cls._create()\n   299→        return cls._singleton\n   300→\n   301→    # ------------------------------------------------------------------#\n   302→    # Instance-level lifecycle\n   303→    # ------------------------------------------------------------------#\n   304→    @classmethod\n   305→    def _create(cls) -> \"EventBus\":\n   306→        # 1. Spawn or attach to broker\n   307→        inbound: _InboundQueue = Queue()\n   308→        shutdown_evt: MpEvent = MpEvent()\n   309→\n   310→        broker = _Broker(inbound, shutdown_evt)\n   311→        broker.start()\n   312→\n   313→        # 2. Register via SyncManager so that we can obtain our *private* queue\n   314→        manager = _BusManager(\n   315→            address=(\"localhost\", 0), authkey=uuid.uuid4().hex.encode(\"ascii\")\n   316→        )\n   317→        # The manager itself runs in a sub-process—start it:\n   318→        manager.start()\n   319→        # Now we can register the broker method for remote access\n   320→        manager.register(\"register_consumer\", broker.register_consumer)  # type: ignore[arg-type]\n   321→\n   322→        consumer_q: _OutboundQueue = manager.register_consumer()  # type: ignore[attr-defined]\n   323→\n   324→        return cls(\n   325→            inbound=inbound,\n   326→            outbound=consumer_q,\n   327→            shutdown_evt=shutdown_evt,\n   328→            broker=broker,\n   329→            manager=manager,\n   330→        )\n   331→\n   332→    # ------------------------------------------------------------------#\n   333→    # dunder\n   334→    # ------------------------------------------------------------------#\n   335→    def __init__(\n   336→        self,\n   337→        *,\n   338→        inbound: _InboundQueue,\n   339→        outbound: _OutboundQueue,\n   340→        shutdown_evt: MpEvent,\n   341→        broker: _Broker,\n   342→        manager: _BusManager,\n   343→    ) -> None:\n   344→        self._inbound = inbound\n   345→        self._outbound = outbound\n   346→        self._broker = broker\n   347→        self._shutdown_evt = shutdown_evt\n   348→        self._manager = manager\n   349→\n   350→        self._handlers: _HandlerMap = {}\n   351→        self._loop = asyncio.get_event_loop_policy().get_event_loop()\n   352→        self._dispatch_task: asyncio.Task[None] | None = None\n   353→\n   354→        # Protect _handlers as multiple event-loop threads could touch it\n   355→        self._h_lock = asyncio.Lock()\n   356→\n   357→        # Synchronize start\n   358→        self._ensure_dispatcher()\n   359→\n   360→    # ------------------------------------------------------------------#\n   361→    # Public API\n   362→    # ------------------------------------------------------------------#\n   363→    def subscribe(self, topic: str, handler: Handler) -> _Subscription:\n   364→        \"\"\"\n   365→        Register *handler* for the given *topic* pattern.\n   366→\n   367→        Wildcards: Use ``'*'`` to match one segment, ``'**'`` to match\n   368→        anything recursively (see :pymod:`fnmatch`).\n   369→\n   370→        Returns\n   371→        -------\n   372→        _Subscription\n   373→            A tiny handle that can be used to *unsubscribe* again.\n   374→        \"\"\"\n   375→        if not callable(handler):\n   376→            raise TypeError(\"Handler must be callable\")\n   377→\n   378→        _log.debug(\"Subscribing %s to topic=%s\", handler, topic)\n   379→\n   380→        # Keep sync handler as is, wrap non-coroutine functions so that we can\n   381→        # `await` them uniformly.\n   382→        if not inspect.iscoroutinefunction(handler):\n   383→\n   384→            async def _async_adapter(evt: Event, _h=handler) -> None:\n   385→                await self._loop.run_in_executor(None, _h, evt)\n   386→\n   387→            adapted: Handler = _async_adapter\n   388→        else:\n   389→            adapted = handler  # type: ignore[assignment]\n   390→\n   391→        # Insert\n   392→        async def _insert() -> None:\n   393→            async with self._h_lock:\n   394→                self._handlers.setdefault(topic, []).append(adapted)\n   395→\n   396→        asyncio.run_coroutine_threadsafe(_insert(), self._loop).result()\n   397→        return _Subscription(topic, adapted, self)\n   398→\n   399→    def publish(self, topic: str, payload: Any, headers: Optional[Mapping[str, Any]] = None) -> None:\n   400→        \"\"\"\n   401→        Fire-and-forget publish.  Safe to call from **any** thread—will enqueue\n   402→        the event into the broker’s *inbound* queue.\n   403→        \"\"\"\n   404→        evt = Event(topic=topic, payload=payload, headers=headers or {})\n   405→        try:\n   406→            self._inbound.put_nowait(evt)\n   407→        except Exception as exc:  # noqa: BLE001\n   408→            _log.exception(\"Failed to publish event %s: %s\", evt, exc)\n   409→\n   410→    async def request(\n   411→        self,\n   412→        topic: str,\n   413→        payload: Any,\n   414→        *,\n   415→        timeout: float = 5.0,\n   416→        headers: Optional[Mapping[str, Any]] = None,\n   417→    ) -> Event:\n   418→        \"\"\"\n   419→        Send a *request* and await the first reply matching the\n   420→        ``correlation_id`` inside *timeout* seconds.\n   421→\n   422→        The server side should look at the ``reply_to`` header and publish the\n   423→        answer to that topic including the ``correlation_id``.\n   424→        \"\"\"\n   425→        correlation_id = uuid.uuid4().hex\n   426→        reply_topic = f\"__reply__.{correlation_id}\"\n   427→\n   428→        fut: asyncio.Future[Event] = self._loop.create_future()\n   429→\n   430→        # Local handler just completes the future\n   431→        async def _reply_handler(evt: Event) -> None:\n   432→            fut.set_result(evt)\n   433→\n   434→        subscription = self.subscribe(reply_topic, _reply_handler)\n   435→\n   436→        # Publish *request*\n   437→        hdrs: Dict[str, Any] = dict(headers or {})\n   438→        hdrs[\"reply_to\"] = reply_topic\n   439→        hdrs[\"correlation_id\"] = correlation_id\n   440→        self.publish(topic, payload, hdrs)\n   441→\n   442→        try:\n   443→            return await asyncio.wait_for(fut, timeout=timeout)\n   444→        except asyncio.TimeoutError as exc:\n   445→            raise HandlerTimeout(f\"Request timed out after {timeout}s\") from exc\n   446→        finally:\n   447→            subscription.unsubscribe()\n   448→\n   449→    # ------------------------------------------------------------------#\n   450→    # Shutdown\n   451→    # ------------------------------------------------------------------#\n   452→    def close(self) -> None:\n   453→        \"\"\"\n   454→        Close the bus, stop the background tasks and terminate the broker.\n   455→\n   456→        **Important:** This method should be called exactly *once* at\n   457→        application shutdown.\n   458→        \"\"\"\n   459→        _log.info(\"Shutting down EventBus in process=%d\", threading.get_native_id())\n   460→\n   461→        self._shutdown_evt.set()\n   462→\n   463→        if self._dispatch_task:\n   464→            self._dispatch_task.cancel()\n   465→\n   466→        # Give the broker a moment to finish pending broadcasts\n   467→        if self._broker.is_alive():\n   468→            self._broker.join(timeout=1.0)\n   469→        self._manager.shutdown()\n   470→\n   471→    # The implementation purposefully avoids external dependencies and\nrelies solely on the standard library (``asyncio``/``multiprocessing``) so that\nplugins do not need to vendor additional wheels.\n\nDesign goals\n============\n\n1. Publish / subscribe with topic-based routing and Unix‐shell wild-card\n   matching (``fnmatch``) so that a handler can listen to groups\n   (e.g. ``\"presence.*\"``).\n\n2. Support both synchronous **and** asynchronous handlers without forcing plugin\n   authors to learn ``asyncio``.\n\n3. Provide a *request / reply* pattern with correlation-IDs and automatic\n   timeout handling—essential for client–server style interactions.\n\n4. Remain safe across processes.  A *broker* process (spawned lazily and managed\n   via :pymod:`multiprocessing.managers`) forwards events between producer and\n   consumer processes transparently.\n\n5. Offer graceful shutdown semantics so that the application can quit quickly\n   even under heavy traffic.\n\nThe broker architecture may feel heavyweight for local desktop IPC, but it\nguarantees that crashing micro-front-ends cannot bring down the other windows.\n\"\"\"\n\nfrom __future__ import annotations\n\nimport asyncio\nimport contextlib\nimport fnmatch\nimport inspect\nimport logging\nimport queue\nimport threading\nimport time\nimport uuid\nfrom dataclasses import dataclass, field\nfrom multiprocessing import Event as MpEvent\nfrom multiprocessing import Process, Queue\nfrom multiprocessing.managers import BaseManager\nfrom types import TracebackType\nfrom typing import (\n    Any,\n    Awaitable,\n    Callable,\n    Dict,\n    List,\n    Mapping,\n    MutableMapping,\n    Optional,\n    Tuple,\n    Type,\n    Union,\n)\n\n# -----------------------------------------------------------------------------\n# Logging\n# -----------------------------------------------------------------------------\n_log = logging.getLogger(\"flockdesk.ipc.event_bus\")\n_log.addHandler(logging.NullHandler())\n\n# -----------------------------------------------------------------------------\n# Exceptions\n# -----------------------------------------------------------------------------\n\n\nclass EventBusError(RuntimeError):\n    \"\"\"Base-class for all event-bus exceptions.\"\"\"\n\n\nclass HandlerTimeout(EventBusError):\n    \"\"\"Raised by ``request`` when no reply arrived in time.\"\"\"\n\n\n# -----------------------------------------------------------------------------\n# Event primitives\n# -----------------------------------------------------------------------------\n\n\n@dataclass(slots=True, frozen=True)\nclass Event:\n    \"\"\"\n    A single immutable message traveling through the *in-memory* event bus.\n\n    Attributes\n    ----------\n    topic:\n        Hierarchical, ``dot``-separated topic name\n        (e.g. ``\"presence.updated\"``).\n    payload:\n        Opaque data Must be *pickleable* because the\n        broker relies on :pymod:`multiprocessing.Queue`.\n    headers:\n        Optional free-form metadata such as authentication tokens or tracing\n        information.  Header values must again be pickleable.\n    event_id:\n        Unique identifier for tracing/debugging purposes.\n    timestamp:\n        Epoch seconds (float) at creation time.\n    origin_pid:\n        The operating-system process identifier of the sender.  Helpful when the\n        same user runs multiple instances of FlockDesk on the same machine.\n    \"\"\"\n\n    topic: str\n    payload: Any\n    headers: Mapping[str, Any] = field(default_factory=dict)\n    event_id: str = field(default_factory=lambda: uuid.uuid4().hex, init=False)\n    timestamp: float = field(default_factory=time.time, init=False)\n    origin_pid: int = field(default_factory=lambda: threading.get_native_id(), init=False)\n\n    def __post_init__(self) -> None:  # noqa: D401\n        \"\"\"Validate that the topic is non-empty.\"\"\"\n        if not self.topic or not isinstance(self.topic, str):\n            raise ValueError(\"Event.topic must be a non-empty string\")\n\n\n# -----------------------------------------------------------------------------\n# Subscription Handle\n# -----------------------------------------------------------------------------\n\n\nclass _Subscription:\n    \"\"\"\n    Lightweight handle that allows clients to stop listening without keeping\n    implementation details around.  Instances can also be used as *async*\n    context-managers.\n\n    NOTE: Users are **not** expected to instantiate this class directly.\n    \"\"\"\n\n    __slots__ = (\"_topic\", \"_handler\", \"_bus\", \"_active\")\n\n    def __init__(\n        self,\n        topic: str,\n        handler: \"Handler\",\n        bus: \"EventBus\",\n    ) -> None:\n        self._topic = topic\n        self._handler = handler\n        self._bus = bus\n        self._active = True\n\n    async def __aenter__(self) -> \"_Subscription\":\n        return self\n\n    async def __aexit__(\n        self,\n        exc_type: Optional[Type[BaseException]],\n        exc: Optional[BaseException],\n        tb: Optional[TracebackType],\n    ) -> None:\n        self.unsubscribe()\n\n    def unsubscribe(self) -> None:\n        \"\"\"Remove the handler from the bus.\"\"\"\n        if self._active:\n            self._bus._remove_handler(self._topic, self._handler)  # alias for readability\n_OutboundQueue = Queue\n\n\nclass _Broker(Process):\n    \"\"\"\n    Spin-up a separate process that forwards a single *inbound* queue to many\n    *outbound* queues (fan-out).\n\n    Each process that calls ``EventBus.connect()`` will create its own outbound\n    queue, while the producing bus writes to a single inbound queue.\n\n    Diagram::\n\n        ┌────────────────────┐\n        │  client process A  │──┐\n        └────────────────────┘  │  ┌────────────┐\n                                ├─▶│            │\n        ┌────────────────────┐  │  │            │\n        │  client process B  │──┼─▶│   BROKER   │──▶ Inbound\n        └────────────────────┘  │  │            │    Queue\n                                └─▶│            │\n                                   └────────────┘\n\n    \"\"\"\n\n    def __init__(self, inbound: _InboundQueue, shutdown_evt: MpEvent):\n        super().__init__(name=\"FlockDeskEventBroker\", daemon=True)\n        self._inbound: _InboundQueue = inbound\n        self._shutdown_evt: MpEvent = shutdown_evt\n        self._outbounds: List[_OutboundQueue] = []\n\n    # ------------------------------------------------------------------#\n    # Public API for other processes (via SyncManager)\n    # ------------------------------------------------------------------#\n    def register_consumer(self) -> _OutboundQueue:\n        \"\"\"\n        Called by a client process to obtain a dedicated outbound queue.\n        \"\"\"\n        q: _OutboundQueue = Queue()\n        self._outbounds.append(q)\n        _log.debug(\"Broker registered new consumer queue (total=%d)\", len(self._outbounds))\n        return q\n\n    # ------------------------------------------------------------------#\n    # Main loop\n    # ------------------------------------------------------------------#\n    def run(self) -> None:  # noqa: D401\n        _log.info(\"Event-bus broker running (PID=%d)\", self.pid)\n        try:\n            while not self._shutdown_evt.is_set():\n                try:\n                    evt: Event = self._inbound.get(timeout=0.1)\n                except queue.Empty:\n                    continue\n\n                # Broadcast\n                for q in self._outbounds:\n                    try:\n                        q.put_nowait(evt)\n                    except Exception:  # noqa: BLE001\n                        _log.exception(\"Failed to forward event to queue %s\", q)\n        finally:\n            _log.info(\"Event-bus broker shutting down (PID=%d)\", self.pid)\n\n\n# -----------------------------------------------------------------------------\n# Multiprocessing manager that exposes broker methods\n# -----------------------------------------------------------------------------\n\n\nclass _BusManager(BaseManager):\n    pass\n\n\n# have to register after class definition\n_BusManager.register(\"register_consumer\", callable=None)  # type: ignore[arg-type]\n\n\n# -----------------------------------------------------------------------------\n# EventBus (public)\n# -----------------------------------------------------------------------------\nclass EventBus:\n    \"\"\"\n    Public entry point used by every micro-front-end.\n\n    Processes talk to a shared broker, threads inside the same process share\n    exactly one :class:`EventBus` instance (thread-safe singleton).\n    \"\"\"\n\n    _singleton: \"EventBus\" | None = None\n    _singleton_lock = threading.Lock()\n\n    # ------------------------------------------------------------------#\n    # Construction helpers\n    # ------------------------------------------------------------------#\n    @classmethod\n    def connect(cls) -> \"EventBus\":\n        \"\"\"\n        Return a *singleton* instance of :class:`EventBus` for the current\n        process.  Lazily starts the broker **once** per workstation.\n        \"\"\"\n        with cls._singleton_lock:\n            if cls._singleton is None:\n                cls._singleton = cls._create()\n        return cls._singleton\n\n    # ------------------------------------------------------------------#\n    # Instance-level lifecycle\n    # ------------------------------------------------------------------#\n    @classmethod\n    def _create(cls) -> \"EventBus\":\n        # 1. Spawn or attach to broker\n        inbound: _InboundQueue = Queue()\n        shutdown_evt: MpEvent = MpEvent()\n\n        broker = _Broker(inbound, shutdown_evt)\n        broker.start()\n\n        # 2. Register via SyncManager so that we can obtain our *private* queue\n        manager = _BusManager(\n            address=(\"localhost\", 0), authkey=uuid.uuid4().hex.encode(\"ascii\")\n        )\n        # The manager itself runs in a sub-process—start it:\n        manager.start()\n        # Now we can register the broker method for remote access\n        manager.register(\"register_consumer\", broker.register_consumer)  # type: ignore[arg-type]\n\n        consumer_q: _OutboundQueue = manager.register_consumer()  # type: ignore[attr-defined]\n\n        return cls(\n            inbound=inbound,\n            outbound=consumer_q,\n            shutdown_evt=shutdown_evt,\n            broker=broker,\n            manager=manager,\n        )\n\n    # ------------------------------------------------------------------#\n    # dunder\n    # ------------------------------------------------------------------#\n    def __init__(\n        self,\n        *,\n        inbound: _InboundQueue,\n        outbound: _OutboundQueue,\n        shutdown_evt: MpEvent,\n        broker: _Broker,\n        manager: _BusManager,\n    ) -> None:\n        self._inbound = inbound\n        self._outbound = outbound\n        self._broker = broker\n        self._shutdown_evt = shutdown_evt\n        self._manager = manager\n\n        self._handlers: _HandlerMap = {}\n        self._loop = asyncio.get_event_loop_policy().get_event_loop()\n        self._dispatch_task: asyncio.Task[None] | None = None\n\n        # Protect _handlers as multiple event-loop threads could touch it\n        self._h_lock = asyncio.Lock()\n\n        # Synchronize start\n        self._ensure_dispatcher()\n\n    # ------------------------------------------------------------------#\n    # Public API\n    # ------------------------------------------------------------------#\n    def subscribe(self, topic: str, handler: Handler) -> _Subscription:\n        \"\"\"\n        Register *handler* for the given *topic* pattern.\n\n        Wildcards: Use ``'*'`` to match one segment, ``'**'`` to match\n        anything recursively (see :pymod:`fnmatch`).\n\n        Returns\n        -------\n        _Subscription\n            A tiny handle that can be used to *unsubscribe* again.\n        \"\"\"\n        if not callable(handler):\n            raise TypeError(\"Handler must be callable\")\n\n        _log.debug(\"Subscribing %s to topic=%s\", handler, topic)\n\n        # Keep sync handler as is, wrap non-coroutine functions so that we can\n        # `await` them uniformly.\n        if not inspect.iscoroutinefunction(handler):\n\n            async def _async_adapter(evt: Event, _h=handler) -> None:\n                await self._loop.run_in_executor(None, _h, evt)\n\n            adapted: Handler = _async_adapter\n        else:\n            adapted = handler  # Safe to call from **any** thread—will enqueue\n        the event into the broker’s *inbound* queue.\n        \"\"\"\n        evt = Event(topic=topic, payload=payload, headers=headers or {})\n        try:\n            self._inbound.put_nowait(evt)\n        except Exception as exc:  # Local handler just completes the future\n        async def _reply_handler(evt: Event) -> None:\n            fut.set_result(evt)\n\n        subscription = self.subscribe(reply_topic, _reply_handler)\n\n        # Publish *request*\n        hdrs: Dict[str, Any] = dict(headers or {})\n        hdrs[\"reply_to\"] = reply_topic\n        hdrs[\"correlation_id\"] = correlation_id\n        self.publish(topic, payload, hdrs)\n\n        try:\n            return await asyncio.wait_for(fut, timeout=timeout)\n        except asyncio.TimeoutError as exc:\n            raise HandlerTimeout(f\"Request timed out after {timeout}s\") from exc\n        finally:\n            subscription.unsubscribe()\n\n    # ------------------------------------------------------------------#\n    # Shutdown\n    # ------------------------------------------------------------------#\n    def close(self) -> None:\n        \"\"\"\n        Close the bus, stop the background tasks and terminate the broker.\n\n        **Important:** This method should be called exactly *once* at\n        application shutdown.\n        \"\"\"\n        _log.info(\"Shutting down EventBus in process=%d\", threading.get_native_id())\n\n        self._shutdown_evt.set()\n\n        if self._dispatch_task:\n            self._dispatch_task.cancel()\n\n        # Give the broker a moment to finish pending broadcasts\n        if self._broker.is_alive():\n            self._broker.join(timeout=1.0)\n        self._manager.shutdown()\n\n    # ------------------------------------------------------------------#\n    # Internal helpers\n    # ------------------------------------------------------------------#\n    def _remove_handler(self, topic: str, handler: Handler) -> None:\n        async def _remove() -> None:\n            async with self._h_lock:\n                lst = self._handlers.get(topic, [])\n                if handler in lst:\n                    lst.remove(handler)\n\n        asyncio.run_coroutine_threadsafe(_remove(), self._loop).result()\n\n    def _ensure_dispatcher(self) -> None:\n        if self._dispatch_task is None or self._dispatch_task.done():\n            self._dispatch_task = self._loop.create_task(self._dispatcher())\n\n    async def _dispatcher(self) -> None:\n        \"\"\"\n        Background coroutine that consumes events from the *outbound* queue and\n        delivers them to pattern-matched handlers.\n        \"\"\"\n        _log.debug(\"EventBus dispatcher started (thread=%s)\", threading.current_thread().name)\n        while not self._shutdown_evt.is_set():\n            try:\n                # Non-blocking read in *executor* because Queue.get is blocking but\n                # not async aware.\n                evt: Event = await self._loop.run_in_executor(None, self._outbound.get, True, 0.1)\n            except queue.Empty:\n                continue\n            except Exception:  # noqa: BLE001\n                _log.exception(\"Unhandled exception while reading outbound queue\")\n                continue\n\n            await self._dispatch(evt)\n\n    async def _dispatch(self, evt: Event) -> None:\n        \"\"\"Match patterns and execute handlers concurrently.\"\"\"\n        async with self._h_lock:\n            # Flatten into (topic_pattern, handlers[])\n            items: List[Tuple[str, List[Handler]]] = list(self._handlers.items())\n\n        coros: List[Awaitable[None]] = []\n\n        for pattern, handlers in items:\n            if fnmatch.fnmatchcase(evt.topic, pattern):\n                coros.extend(h(evt) for h in handlers)\n\n        if not coros:\n            return  # no listeners\n\n        await asyncio.gather(*coros, return_exceptions=False)\n\n    # ------------------------------------------------------------------#\n    # Context manager sugar\n    # ------------------------------------------------------------------#\n    def __enter__(self) -> \"EventBus\":\n        return self\n\n    def __exit__(\n        self,\n        exc_type: Optional[Type[BaseException]],\n        exc: Optional[BaseException],\n        tb: Optional[TracebackType],\n    ) -> None:\n        self.close()\n\n\n# -----------------------------------------------------------------------------\n# Convenience helpers for library-wide singleton access\n# -----------------------------------------------------------------------------\n\n_bus: EventBus | None = None\n_bus_lock = threading.Lock()\n\n\ndef get_event_bus() -> EventBus:\n    \"\"\"\n    Return a process-global :class:`EventBus` instance, creating it on first use.\n    \"\"\"\n    global _bus\n    with _bus_lock:\n        if _bus is None:\n            _bus = EventBus.connect()\n        return _bus\n\n\n# -----------------------------------------------------------------------------\n# Example usage (guarded)\n# -----------------------------------------------------------------------------\nif __name__ == \"__main__\":  # pragma: no cover\n    logging.basicConfig(level=logging.DEBUG)\n\n    async def main() -> None:\n        bus = get_event_bus()\n\n        # Example async handler\n        async def on_presence(evt: Event) -> None:\n            print(\"[async] received\", evt.topic, evt.payload)\n\n        # Example sync handler\n        def on_any(evt: Event) -> None:\n            print(\"[sync ] saw\", evt.topic, evt.payload)\n\n        bus.subscribe(\"presence.*\", on_presence)\n        bus.subscribe(\"**\", on_any)\n\n        # Publish from different thread\n        def _publisher() -> None:\n            b = get_event_bus()\n            b.publish(\"presence.updated\", {\"user\": \"alice\", \"status\": \"online\"})\n\n        threading.Thread(target=_publisher, daemon=True).start()\n\n        await asyncio.sleep(1.0)\n        bus.close()\n\n    asyncio.run(main())\n```","numLines":588,"startLine":1,"totalLines":588}}}

   \"\"\"\n   127→        Send a message to the current channel.\n   128→\n   129→        The actual network interaction is delegated to the domain layer\n   130→        via the event-bus. -------- Qt Signals ----------------------------------------------------#\n    messageAdded = QtCore.Signal(ChatMessage)  # new message appended\n    messageReplaced = QtCore.Signal(ChatMessage)  # edited or updated\n    typingStatusChanged = QtCore.Signal(str, bool)  # -----------------------------------------------------------------------#\n    # API surface for Controllers\n    # -----------------------------------------------------------------------#\n    def history(self) -> list[ChatMessage]:\n        \"\"\"Return a copy of the current message history.\"\"\"\n        with self._lock:\n            return list(self._messages)\n\n    def send_message(self, content: str, attachments: Optional[List[Path]] = None) -> None:\n        \"\"\"\n        Send a message to the current channel.\n\n        The actual network interaction is delegated to the domain layer\n        via the event-bus. We optimistically append the message locally\n        and mark it as `is_outgoing=True`; once the server acknowledges\n        (or rejects) the message we will replace/update it.\n        \"\"\"\n        if not content and not attachments:\n            _log.debug(\"Empty message suppressed\")\n            return\n\n        msg_id = f\"tmp-{datetime.now(tz=timezone.utc).timestamp()}\"\n        attachments = attachments or []\n        outgoing = ChatMessage(\n            msg_id=msg_id,\n            channel_id=self._channel_id,\n            sender_id=settings.get(\"user_id\", \"anonymous\"),\n            sender_display=settings.get(\"user_display\", \"Me\"),\n            content=content,\n            timestamp=datetime.now(tz=timezone.utc),\n            is_outgoing=True,\n            attachments=attachments,\n        )\n\n        self._append_message(outgoing)\n\n        # Clear persisted draft for this channel\n        self._clear_draft_file()\n\n    def start_typing(self) -> None:\n        \"\"\"Publish typing indicator event when user starts typing.\"\"\"\n        self._event_bus.publish(\n            Event(\n                topic=\"chat.typing\",\n                payload={\"channel_id\": self._channel_id, \"user_id\": settings.get(\"user_id\")},\n            )\n        )\n\n    def persist_draft(self, draft_text: str) -> None:\n        \"\"\"\n        Persist unsent draft on disk.\n\n        Called from the Controller on text-changed events.\n        \"\"\"\n        if not draft_text.strip():\n            self._clear_draft_file()\n            return\n\n        self._draft_path.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)\n        try:\n            self._draft_path.write_text(\n                json.dumps({\"draft\": draft_text, \"timestamp\": datetime.now().isoformat()}),\n                encoding=\"utf-8\",\n            )\n            _log.debug(\"Draft persisted to %s\", self._draft_path)\n        except OSError as exc:\n            _log.warning(\"Failed to write draft file: %s\", exc)\n\n    # -----------------------------------------------------------------------#\n    # Internal helpers\n    # -----------------------------------------------------------------------#\n    def _register_event_subscriptions(self) -> None:\n        \"\"\"\n        Subscribe to the global EventBus for chat-related events.\n\n        We keep the subscription tokens so we can unsubscribe\n        when the View-Model is deleted / the window is closed.\n        \"\"\"\n\n        # Incoming messages\n        self._event_bus.subscribe(\n            \"chat.incoming\",\n            self._on_incoming_message,\n            group=f\"vm_{self._channel_id}\",\n        )\n\n        # Delivery acknowledgements for optimistic updates\n        self._event_bus.subscribe(\n            \"chat.outgoing.confirmation\",\n            self._on_delivery_confirmation,\n            group=f\"vm_{self._channel_id}\",\n        )\n\n        # Typing indicators\n        self._event_bus.subscribe(\n            \"chat.typing\",\n            self._on_typing_indicator,\n            group=f\"vm_{self._channel_id}\",\n        )\n\n        # Synchronised edits / deletions\n        self._event_bus.subscribe(\n            \"chat.message.updated\",\n            self._on_remote_update,\n            group=f\"vm_{self._channel_id}\",\n        )\n\n    # -----------------------------------------------------------------------#\n    def _append_message(self, message: ChatMessage) -> None:\n        with self._lock:\n            self._messages.append(message)\n        self.messageAdded.emit(message)\n\n    # -----------------------------------------------------------------------#\n    # Event-bus callback handlers\n    # -----------------------------------------------------------------------#\n    def _on_incoming_message(self, event: Event) -> None:\n        \"\"\"Handle new message events from other users.\"\"\"\n        payload = event.payload\n        if payload[\"channel_id\"] != self._channel_id:\n            return\n\n        msg = ChatMessage(\n            msg_id=payload[\"msg_id\"],\n            channel_id=payload[\"channel_id\"],\n            sender_id=payload[\"sender_id\"],\n            sender_display=payload[\"sender_display\"],\n            content=payload[\"content\"],\n            timestamp=datetime.fromisoformat(payload[\"timestamp\"]),\n            attachments=[Path(p) for p in payload.get(\"attachments\", [])],\n        )\n        self._append_message(msg)\n\n    # -----------------------------------------------------------------------#\n    def _on_delivery_confirmation(self, event: Event) -> None:\n        \"\"\"Replace optimistic message with definitive server copy.\"\"\"\n        payload = event.payload\n        if payload[\"channel_id\"] != self._channel_id:\n            return\n\n        tmp_id = payload[\"tmp_id\"]\n        real_id = payload[\"msg_id\"]\n\n        with self._lock:\n            for idx, m in enumerate(self._messages):\n                if m.msg_id == tmp_id:\n                    updated = ChatMessage(\n                        msg_id=real_id,\n                        channel_id=m.channel_id,\n                        sender_id=m.sender_id,\n                        sender_display=m.sender_display,\n                        content=m.content,\n                        timestamp=datetime.fromisoformat(payload[\"timestamp\"]),\n                        is_outgoing=False,\n                        attachments=m.attachments,\n                    )\n                    self._messages[idx] = updated\n                    _log.debug(\"Optimistic message %s replaced by %s\", tmp_id, real_id)\n                    self.messageReplaced.emit(updated)\n                    break\n\n    # -----------------------------------------------------------------------#\n    def _on_typing_indicator(self, event: Event) -> None:\n        \"\"\"Update typing indicator map & emit signal.\"\"\"\n        payload = event.payload\n        if payload[\"channel_id\"] != self._channel_id:\n            return\n\n        user_id = payload[\"user_id\"]\n        if user_id == settings.get(\"user_id\"):\n            return  # ignore self typing\n\n        # Reset/Start debounce timer for this user\n        timer = self._typing_users.get(user_id)\n        if timer is None:\n            timer = QtCore.QTimer(self)\n            timer.setSingleShot(True)\n            timer.timeout.connect(lambda uid=user_id: self._emit_typing_end(uid))\n            self._typing_users[user_id] = timer\n            self.typingStatusChanged.emit(user_id, True)\n\n        timer.start(3000)  # 3s window\n\n    def _emit_typing_end(self, user_id: str) -> None:\n        self.typingStatusChanged.emit(user_id, False)\n        self._typing_users.pop(user_id, None)\n\n    # -----------------------------------------------------------------------#\n    def _on_remote_update(self, event: Event) -> None:\n        \"\"\"Handle remote edits or deletions.\"\"\"\n        payload = event.payload\n        if payload[\"channel_id\"] != self._channel_id:\n            return\n\n        with self._lock:\n            for idx, m in enumerate(self._messages):\n                if m.msg_id == payload[\"msg_id\"]:\n                    updated = ChatMessage(\n                        msg_id=m.msg_id,\n                        channel_id=m.channel_id,\n                        sender_id=m.sender_id,\n                        sender_display=m.sender_display,\n                        content=payload.get(\"content\", m.content),\n                        timestamp=m.timestamp,\n                        edited=True,\n                        attachments=[Path(p) for p in payload.get(\"attachments\", [])],\n                    )\n                    self._messages[idx] = updated\n                    self.messageReplaced.emit(updated)\n                    _log.debug(\"Message %s updated from remote\", m.msg_id)\n                    break\n\n    # -----------------------------------------------------------------------#\n    # Draft persistence\n    # -----------------------------------------------------------------------#\n    def _restore_draft_async(self) -> None:\n        \"\"\"Restore draft in a background thread to keep UI responsive.\"\"\"\n\n        def _worker(vm: \"ChatViewModel\") -> None:\n            if vm._draft_path.exists():\n                try:\n                    data = json.loads(vm._draft_path.read_text(encoding=\"utf-8\"))\n                    draft = data.get(\"draft\", \"\")\n                    _log.debug(\"Draft restored for channel %s\", vm._channel_id)\n                    # Emit via Qt to avoid cross-thread issues\n                    QtCore.QMetaObject.invokeMethod(\n                        vm,\n                        \"_emit_draft_restored\",\n                        QtCore.Qt.QueuedConnection,\n                        QtCore.Q_ARG(str, draft),\n                    )\n                except (OSError, json.JSONDecodeError) as exc:\n                    _log.warning(\"Failed to restore draft: %s\", exc)\n\n        threading.Thread(target=_worker, args=(self,), daemon=True).start()\n\n    @QtCore.Slot(str)\n    def _emit_draft_restored(self, text: str) -> None:\n        self.historyLoaded.emit([])  # just to notify view to attach draft text\n\n    def _clear_draft_file(self) -> None:\n        try:\n            if self._draft_path.exists():\n                self._draft_path.unlink(missing_ok=True)\n                _log.debug(\"Draft cleared for channel %s\", self._channel_id)\n        except OSError as exc:\n            _log.warning(\"Failed to clear draft file: %s\", exc)\n\n    # -----------------------------------------------------------------------#\n    # Qt Object Life-Cycle\n    # -----------------------------------------------------------------------#\n    def __del__(self) -> None:\n        \"\"\"Ensure we cleanly unsubscribe from the EventBus.\"\"\"\n        try:\n            self._event_bus.unsubscribe_group(f\"vm_{self._channel_id}\")\n        except Exception:  # pragma: no cover\n            pass  # During interpreter shutdown objects may be gone\n\n\n# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------#\n# Factory (Dependency Injection ready)\n# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------#\ndef create_chat_viewmodel(channel_id: str, event_bus: EventBus) -> ChatViewModel:\n    \"\"\"\n    Factory helper so the app’s DI container can easily inject dependencies.\n\n    Returns\n    -------\n    ChatViewModel\n        Ready-to-use instance with subscriptions in place.\n    \"\"\"\n    vm = ChatViewModel(channel_id=channel_id, event_bus=event_bus)\n    return vm\n```","numLines":400,"startLine":1,"totalLines":400}}}

Each card is a plugin that exposes a QWidget\nand registers itself to the internal event-bus.  Users can re-arrange,\nremove, and add cards simply by dragging & dropping new plugins from the\nplugin-palette or by right-clicking a card and selecting *Remove*.\n\nDesign goals\n------------\n1. MVVM separation – the widget delegates all non-UI work to the\n   DashboardViewModel.\n2. Event driven – real-time updates via the global EventBus.\n3. Plugin friendly – cards are loaded/unloaded via the PluginManager.\n4. Robustness – every plugin is sandboxed; a faulty widget is replaced\n   with a red *crash card* rather than crashing the entire dashboard.\n\nThe implementation purposefully stays clear of concrete business-logic.\nProjects embedding this component must provide:\n\n* flockdesk.core.event_bus.EventBus              (pub/sub)\n* flockdesk.core.plugins.manager.PluginManager   (plugin life-cycle)\n* flockdesk.core.settings.AppSettings            (persistent settings)\n\"\"\"\n\nfrom __future__ import annotations\n\nimport asyncio\nimport logging\nimport traceback\nfrom contextlib import Connect signals\n        self._vm.themeChanged.connect(self._apply_theme)\n        self._vm.settingsChanged.connect(self._rebuild_cards)\n        self._vm.cardCrashed.connect(self._handle_card_crash)\n\n        # Initial population\n        self._vm.load_initial_cards()\n        self._rebuild_cards()\n\n    # ---------------------------------------------------------------- UI init\n\n    def _build_ui(self) -> None:\n        self._grid = QGridLayout()\n        self._grid.setSpacing(12)\n        self._grid.setContentsMargins(12, 12, 12, 12)\n        self.setLayout(self._grid)\n\n    # ------------------------------------------------------------ Card helpers\n\n    def _handle_card_crash(self, plugin_id: str) -> None:\n        logger.debug(\"Handling crash of plugin '%s'\", plugin_id)\n        crash_card = CrashCard(plugin_id)\n        self._insert_widget(crash_card)\n\n    def _rebuild_cards(self) -> None:\n        \"\"\"\n        Re-create the grid whenever cards were added/removed or when a plugin\n        crashed.\n        \"\"\"\n        while self._grid.count():\n            item = self._grid.takeAt(0)\n            if widget := item.widget():\n                widget.setParent(None)\n                widget.deleteLater()\n\n        col, row = 0, 0\n        for meta in self._vm.cards().values():\n            self._insert_widget(meta.widget, row=row, col=col)\n            col += 1\n            if col >= self.GRID_COLUMNS:\n                col = 0\n                row += 1\n\n        self._grid.setRowStretch(row + 1, 1)\n        self._grid.setColumnStretch(self.GRID_COLUMNS, 1)\n        self.updateGeometry()\n\n    def _insert_widget(self, widget: QWidget, *, row: int | None = None, col: int | None = None) -> None:\n        \"\"\"\n        Insert `widget` into the next free cell unless `row/col` is provided.\n        \"\"\"\n        if row is None or col is None:\n            position = self._next_free_cell()\n            row, col = position\n\n        widget.setSizePolicy(QSizePolicy.Expanding, QSizePolicy.Expanding)\n        self._grid.addWidget(widget, row, col)\n        logger.debug(\"Inserted widget at row=%s col=%s\", row, col)\n\n    def _next_free_cell(self) -> tuple[int, int]:\n        \"\"\"\n        Calculate the next free slot in the grid using a simple left-to-right,\n        top-to-bottom algorithm.\n        \"\"\"\n        count = self._grid.count()\n        col = count % self.GRID_COLUMNS\n        row = count // self.GRID_COLUMNS\n        return row, col\n\n    # ----------------------------------------------------------- Drag & drop\n\n    def dragEnterEvent(self, event: QDragEnterEvent) -> None:\n        mime: QMimeData = event.mimeData()\n        if mime.hasFormat(DashboardViewModel.MIME_TYPE):\n            event.setDropAction(Qt.CopyAction)\n            event.acceptProposedAction()\n        else:\n            super().dragEnterEvent(event)\n\n    def dropEvent(self, event: QDropEvent) -> None:\n        mime: QMimeData = event.mimeData()\n        if not mime.hasFormat(DashboardViewModel.MIME_TYPE):\n            super().dropEvent(event)\n            return\n\n        plugin_id_bytes = mime.data(DashboardViewModel.MIME_TYPE)\n        plugin_id = bytes(plugin_id_bytes).decode()\n\n        logger.debug(\"Plugin dropped on dashboard: %s\", plugin_id)\n        self._vm.add_card(plugin_id)\n        event.acceptProposedAction()\n\n    # ---------------------------------------------------------- Context menu\n\n    def contextMenuEvent(self, event: QContextMenuEvent) -> None:\n        pos = event.globalPos()\n        widget_at_pos = self.childAt(event.pos())\n\n        menu = QMenu(self)\n        if isinstance(widget_at_pos, CrashCard):\n            plugin_id = self._plugin_id_for_widget(widget_at_pos)\n            if plugin_id:\n                remove_action = QAction(\"Remove card\", self)\n                remove_action.triggered.connect(\n                    lambda _=False, pid=plugin_id: self._vm.remove_card(pid)\n                )\n                menu.addAction(remove_action)\n        elif plugin_id := self._plugin_id_for_widget(widget_at_pos):\n            remove_action = QAction(\"Remove card\", self)\n            remove_action.triggered.connect(\n                lambda _=False, pid=plugin_id: self._vm.remove_card(pid)\n            )\n            settings_action = QAction(\"Card settings…\", self)\n            settings_action.triggered.connect(\n                lambda _=False, pid=plugin_id: self._open_plugin_settings(pid)\n            )\n            menu.addActions([settings_action, remove_action])\n        else:\n            refresh_action = QAction(\"Refresh dashboard\", self)\n            refresh_action.triggered.connect(self._rebuild_cards)\n            menu.addAction(refresh_action)\n\n        menu.exec(pos)\n\n    def _plugin_id_for_widget(self, child: Optional[QWidget]) -> Optional[str]:\n        if child is None:\n            return None\n        for pid, meta in self._vm.cards().items():\n            if meta.widget is child or child.isAncestorOf(meta.widget):\n                return pid\n        return None\n\n    # --------------------------------------------------------------- Helpers\n\n    def _open_plugin_settings(self, plugin_id: str) -> None:\n        \"\"\"Call the plugin’s settings UI or show fallback dialog.\"\"\"\n        plugin = self._vm._plugin_manager.get_plugin(plugin_id)  # pylint: disable=protected-access\n        if hasattr(plugin, \"open_settings\"):  # type: ignore[attr-defined]\n            plugin.open_settings(self)  # type: ignore[attr-defined]\n        else:\n            QMessageBox.information(\n                self,\n                \"Settings\",\n                f\"Plugin '{plugin_id}' does not expose a settings panel.\",\n            )\n\n    # --------------------------------------------------------------- Theming\n\n    @Slot(str)\n    def _apply_theme(self, theme_name: str) -> None:  # noqa: D401\n        \"\"\"\n        Apply QSS from the theme directory.  Blocks the UI minimally thanks to\n        async file I/O.\n        \"\"\"\n\n        async def _load_qss(name: str) -> str:  # noqa: D401\n            theme_dir = Path(QApplication.instance().property(\"themePath\"))  # type: ignore[arg-type]\n            qss_file = theme_dir / f\"{name}.qss\"\n            try:\n                return qss_file.read_text(encoding=\"utf-8\")\n            except FileNotFoundError:\n                logger.warning(\"Theme file missing: %s\", qss_file)\n                return \"\"\n\n        async def _apply() -> None:  # noqa: D401\n            qss = await _load_qss(theme_name)\n            QApplication.instance().setStyleSheet(qss)\n\n        asyncio.create_task(_apply(), name=\"dashboard.apply_theme\")\n\n    # ---------------------------------------------------------- Event filter\n\n    def event(self, e: QEvent):  # noqa: D401, N802\n        \"\"\"\n        Override event() to intercept plugin widget crashes.  Qt does not\n        natively raise Python exceptions that occur during event processing\n        inside child widgets.  By installing a global *except-hook* we can at\n        least replace the faulty widget with a CrashCard.\n        \"\"\"\n        try:\n            return super().event(e)\n        except Exception:  # noqa: BLE001\n            traceback.print_exc()\n            crashed_widget = self.childAt(self.mapFromGlobal(QCursor.pos()))\n            plugin_id = self._plugin_id_for_widget(crashed_widget)\n            if plugin_id:\n                self._handle_card_crash(plugin_id)\n            return True  # Event handled\n\n\n# -----------------------------------------------------------------------------\n# Stand-alone test shim\n# -----------------------------------------------------------------------------\n\nif __name__ == \"__main__\":\n    import sys\n\n    logging.basicConfig(level=logging.DEBUG)\n\n    app = QApplication(sys.argv)\n    dash = DashboardWidget()\n    dash.resize(QSize(900, 600))\n    dash.show()\n\n    sys.exit(app.exec())\n```","numLines":458,"startLine":1,"totalLines":458}}}

implementation\n```\n\n### Event Types Registration Pattern\n```python\n// File: /app/project/flockdesk/core/ipc/event_types.py\n// Lines: 57-80\n\nclass EventTopic(str, Enum):\n    \"\"\"\n    Logical top-level buckets for events flowing through the FlockDesk bus.\n    \"\"\"\n    CHAT = \"chat\"\n    WHITEBOARD = \"whiteboard\"\n    PRESENCE = \"presence\"\n    FILES = \"files\"\n    SETTINGS = \"settings\"\n    PLUGIN = \"plugin\"\n    SYSTEM = \"system\"\n    LOGGING = \"logging\"\n    METRICS = \"metrics\"\n```\n\n### Settings Service Pattern\n```python\n// File: /app/project/flockdesk/core/services/settings_service.py\n// Lines: 231-258\n\ndef set_value(self, namespace: str, key: str, value: Any) -> None:\n    \"\"\"\n    Update a single field in the namespace and enqueue the change for\n    persistence and event-bus broadcast.\n\n    Type mismatches raise immediately.\n    \"\"\"\n    with self._lock:\n        # ... validation and type checking\n        setattr(instance, key, value)\n        self._mark_dirty(namespace)\n```\n\n### Command Registry Pattern\n```python\n// File: /app/project/flockdesk/core/shortcuts/commands.py\n// Lines: 221-262\n\ndef register(\n    self,\n    *,\n    id: str,\n    description: str,\n    sequence: str | QKeySequence,\n    handler: Callable[[], None],\n    enabled: Callable[[], bool] = lambda: True,\n    owner: Optional[str] = None,\n) -> ShortcutCommand:\n    \"\"\"\n    Registers a new command or raises :class:`DuplicateCommandError`.\n    \"\"\"\n    # ... implementation\n```\n\n### Menu Bar Structure\n```python\n// File: /app/project/flockdesk/core/shell/menu_bar.py\n// Lines: 232-234\n\n# View --------------------------------------------------------------\nview_menu = self.addMenu(\"&View\")\ntheme_menu = view_menu.addMenu(\"Theme\")\nself._theme_menu = theme_menu  # used later for dynamic population\n```\n\n### Chat ViewModel Event Subscription self._event_bus.subscribe(\"plugin.unloaded\", self._on_plugin_unloaded),\n        self._event_bus.subscribe(\"theme.external_switch\",\n                                  self._on_external_theme_switch),\n    ])\n```\n\n## Analysis\n\n### Architectural Patterns Identified\n\n1. **Event-Driven Architecture (EventBus)**\n   - The application uses a centralized EventBus (`flockdesk/core/ipc/event_bus.py`) for decoupled communication\n   - Events use topic-based routing with wildcard support\n   - Both sync and async handlers are supported\n   - The EventBus is process-safe via multiprocessing\n\n2. **Service Layer Pattern (SettingsService)**\n   - SettingsService manages all persistent user preferences\n   - Uses dataclass schemas for type-safe settings\n   - Auto-saves with debouncing to avoid excessive disk writes\n   - Broadcasts `settings.updated` events on changes\n\n3. **MVVM Pattern in Modules**\n   - Chat and Dashboard modules follow MVVM architecture\n   - ViewModels contain business logic and subscribe to EventBus\n   - Views bind to ViewModel signals (Qt signals/slots)\n   - Views are passive observers of ViewModel state\n\n4. **Command Pattern**\n   - Commands are registered via `ShortcutCommandRegistry`\n   - Commands encapsulate actions with enable/disable logic\n   - Commands emit events on execution for tracing\n\n### Dependencies and Relationships\n\n```\n┌─────────────────┐     publishes     ┌─────────────────┐\n│   MenuBar       │────────────────────│   EventBus      │\n│  (View Menu)    │                    │                 │\n└─────────────────┘                    └────────┬────────┘\n        │                                       │\n        │ triggers                              │ broadcasts\n        ▼                                       ▼\n┌─────────────────┐                    ┌─────────────────┐\n│ToggleFocusMode  │──────────────────▶│SettingsService  │\n│   Command       │     updates        │                 │\n└─────────────────┘                    └─────────────────┘\n                                                │\n                                                │ persists to\n                                                ▼\n                                       ┌─────────────────┐\n                                       │default_settings │\n                                       │    .json        │\n                                       └─────────────────┘\n\n┌─────────────────┐     subscribes     ┌─────────────────┐\n│  ChatViewModel  │◀───────────────────│   EventBus      │\n│                 │                    │                 │\n└────────┬────────┘                    └─────────────────┘\n         │                                      ▲\n         │ notifies via signals                 │ subscribes\n         ▼                                      │\n┌─────────────────┐                    ┌────────┴────────┐\n│   ChatWidget    │                    │DashboardViewModel│\n│ (Snooze Label)  │                    │                 │\n└─────────────────┘                    └────────┬────────┘\n                                                │\n                                                │ notifies via signals\n                                                ▼\n                                       ┌─────────────────┐\n                                       │DashboardWidget  │\n                                       │(Activity Feed)  │\n                                       └─────────────────┘\n```\n\n## Implementation\n\n### 1. Define FOCUS_MODE_CHANGED Event in event_types.py\n\n**File: `/app/project/flockdesk/core/ipc/event_types.py`**\n\nAdd to EventTopic enum and create the payload dataclass:\n\n```python\n// File: /app/project/flockdesk/core/ipc/event_types.py\n// Lines to add after line 76 (in EventTopic enum):\n\n    # Focus Mode\n    FOCUS = \"focus\"\n\n// Lines to add after line 363 (after SettingsChanged payload):\n\n# --- FOCUS MODE --------------------------------------------------------- #\n\n@event(topic=EventTopic.FOCUS, name=\"mode.changed\")\n@dataclass(slots=True, frozen=True)\nclass FocusModeChanged(_PayloadBase):\n    \"\"\"Payload for focus mode state changes.\"\"\"\n    enabled: bool\n    changed_at: datetime = field(\n        default_factory=lambda: datetime.now(timezone.utc)\n    )\n```\n\n### 3. Create ToggleFocusModeCommand in commands.py\n\n**File: `/app/project/flockdesk/core/shortcuts/commands.py`**\n\nAdd a new command registration for toggling focus mode:\n\n```python\n// File: /app/project/flockdesk/core/shortcuts/commands.py\n// Lines to add in _register_core_commands() function, after line 400:\n\n    def _toggle_focus_mode():\n        \"\"\"Toggle Focus Mode on/off and publish state change.\"\"\"\n        try:\n            from flockdesk.core.services.settings_service import SettingsService\n            from flockdesk.core.ioc import container\n\n            settings_svc: SettingsService = container.resolve(SettingsService)\n            current_state = settings_svc.get(\"gui\").get(\"focus_mode_enabled\", False)\n            new_state = not current_state\n\n            settings_svc.set_value(\"gui\", \"focus_mode_enabled\", new_state)\n\n            # Publish focus mode changed event\n            _bus.emit(\"focus.mode.changed\", enabled=new_state)\n\n            logger.info(\"Focus Mode %s\", \"enabled\" if new_state else \"disabled\")\n        except Exception:\n            logger.exception(\"Failed to toggle focus mode\")\n\n    registry.register(\n        id=\"core.toggle_focus_mode\",\n        description=\"Toggle Focus Mode\",\n        sequence=\"Ctrl+Shift+F\",\n        handler=_toggle_focus_mode,\n        owner=\"flockdesk.core\",\n    )\n```\n\n### 4. Add Toggle Focus Mode Menu Item to menu_bar.py\n\n**File: `/app/project/flockdesk/core/shell/menu_bar.py`**\n\nAdd Focus Mode toggle to the View menu:\n\n```python\n// File: /app/project/flockdesk/core/shell/menu_bar.py\n// Lines to modify in _init_static_structure() method, after line 234:\n\n        # View --------------------------------------------------------------\n        view_menu = self.addMenu(\"&View\")\n\n        # Focus Mode toggle\n        self._focus_mode_action = QAction(\"Toggle Focus Mode\", self, checkable=True)\n        self._focus_mode_action.setShortcut(\"Ctrl+Shift+F\")\n        self._focus_mode_action.setToolTip(\"Enable Focus Mode to reduce distractions\")\n        self._focus_mode_action.triggered.connect(_SafeSlot(self._toggle_focus_mode))\n        view_menu.addAction(self._focus_mode_action)\n        view_menu.addSeparator()\n\n        theme_menu = view_menu.addMenu(\"Theme\")\n        self._theme_menu = theme_menu  # used later for dynamic population\n\n// Add new method after line 368:\n\n    def _toggle_focus_mode(self) -> None:\n        \"\"\"Toggle Focus Mode via the command registry.\"\"\"\n        try:\n            from flockdesk.core.shortcuts.commands import registry\n            registry.trigger(\"core.toggle_focus_mode\")\n            # Update checkbox state based on new setting\n            # The actual state comes from SettingsService\n        except Exception as exc:\n            _logger.error(\"Failed to toggle focus mode: %s\", exc)\n```\n\n### 5.
