(`flockdesk/core/ipc/event_bus.py`) for decoupled communication\n   - Events use topic-based routing with wildcard support\n   - Both sync and async handlers are supported\n   - The EventBus is process-safe via multiprocessing\n\n2. **Service Layer Pattern (SettingsService)**\n   - SettingsService manages all persistent user preferences\n   - Uses dataclass schemas for type-safe settings\n   - Auto-saves with debouncing to avoid excessive disk writes\n   - Broadcasts `settings.updated` events on changes\n\n3. **MVVM Pattern in Modules**\n   - Chat and Dashboard modules follow MVVM architecture\n   - ViewModels contain business logic and subscribe to EventBus\n   - Views bind to ViewModel signals (Qt signals/slots)\n   - Views are passive observers of ViewModel state\n\n4. **Command Pattern**\n   - Commands are registered via `ShortcutCommandRegistry`\n   - Commands encapsulate actions with enable/disable logic\n   - Commands emit events on execution for tracing\n\n### Dependencies and Relationships\n\n```\n┌─────────────────┐     publishes     ┌─────────────────┐\n│   MenuBar       │────────────────────│   EventBus      │\n│  (View Menu)    │                    │                 │\n└─────────────────┘                    └────────┬────────┘\n        │                                       │\n        │ triggers                              │ broadcasts\n        ▼                                       ▼\n┌─────────────────┐                    ┌─────────────────┐\n│ToggleFocusMode  │──────────────────▶│SettingsService  │\n│   Command       │     updates        │                 │\n└─────────────────┘                    └─────────────────┘\n                                                │\n                                                │ persists to\n                                                ▼\n                                       ┌─────────────────┐\n                                       │default_settings │\n                                       │    .json        │\n                                       └─────────────────┘\n\n┌─────────────────┐     subscribes     ┌─────────────────┐\n│  ChatViewModel  │◀───────────────────│   EventBus      │\n│                 │                    │                 │\n└────────┬────────┘                    └─────────────────┘\n         │                                      ▲\n         │ notifies via signals                 │ subscribes\n         ▼                                      │\n┌─────────────────┐                    ┌────────┴────────┐\n│   ChatWidget    │                    │DashboardViewModel│\n│ (Snooze Label)  │                    │                 │\n└─────────────────┘                    └────────┬────────┘\n                                                │\n                                                │ notifies via signals\n                                                ▼\n                                       ┌─────────────────┐\n                                       │DashboardWidget  │\n                                       │(Activity Feed)  │\n                                       └─────────────────┘\n```\n\n## Implementation\n\n### 1. Add Focus Mode Setting to default_settings.json\n\n**File: `/app/project/configs/default_settings.json`**\n\nAdd `focus_mode_enabled` under the `gui` section:\n\n```json\n// File: /app/project/configs/default_settings.json\n// Change: Add focus_mode_enabled to gui section\n\n\"gui\": {\n    \"focus_mode_enabled\": false,\n    \"window\": {\n      ...\n```\n\n### 2. Define FOCUS_MODE_CHANGED Event in event_types.py\n\n**File: `/app/project/flockdesk/core/ipc/event_types.py`**\n\nAdd to EventTopic enum and create the payload dataclass:\n\n```python\n// File: /app/project/flockdesk/core/ipc/event_types.py\n// Lines to add after line 76 (in EventTopic enum):\n\n    # Focus Mode\n    FOCUS = \"focus\"\n\n// Lines to add after line 363 (after SettingsChanged payload):\n\n# --- FOCUS MODE --------------------------------------------------------- #\n\n@event(topic=EventTopic.FOCUS, name=\"mode.changed\")\n@dataclass(slots=True, frozen=True)\nclass FocusModeChanged(_PayloadBase):\n    \"\"\"Payload for focus mode state changes.\"\"\"\n    enabled: bool\n    changed_at: datetime = field(\n        default_factory=lambda: datetime.now(timezone.utc)\n    )\n```\n\n### 3. Create ToggleFocusModeCommand in commands.py\n\n**File: `/app/project/flockdesk/core/shortcuts/commands.py`**\n\nAdd a new command registration for toggling focus mode:\n\n```python\n// File: /app/project/flockdesk/core/shortcuts/commands.py\n// Lines to add in _register_core_commands() function, after line 400:\n\n    def _toggle_focus_mode():\n        \"\"\"Toggle Focus Mode on/off and publish state change.\"\"\"\n        try:\n            from flockdesk.core.services.settings_service import SettingsService\n            from flockdesk.core.ioc import container\n\n            settings_svc: SettingsService = container.resolve(SettingsService)\n            current_state = settings_svc.get(\"gui\").get(\"focus_mode_enabled\", False)\n            new_state = not current_state\n\n            settings_svc.set_value(\"gui\", \"focus_mode_enabled\", new_state)\n\n            # Publish focus mode changed event\n            _bus.emit(\"focus.mode.changed\", enabled=new_state)\n\n            logger.info(\"Focus Mode %s\", \"enabled\" if new_state else \"disabled\")\n        except Exception:\n            logger.exception(\"Failed to toggle focus mode\")\n\n    registry.register(\n        id=\"core.toggle_focus_mode\",\n        description=\"Toggle Focus Mode\",\n        sequence=\"Ctrl+Shift+F\",\n        handler=_toggle_focus_mode,\n        owner=\"flockdesk.core\",\n    )\n```\n\n### 4. Add Toggle Focus Mode Menu Item to menu_bar.py\n\n**File: `/app/project/flockdesk/core/shell/menu_bar.py`**\n\nAdd Focus Mode toggle to the View menu:\n\n```python\n// File: /app/project/flockdesk/core/shell/menu_bar.py\n// Lines to modify in _init_static_structure() method, after line 234:\n\n        # View --------------------------------------------------------------\n        view_menu = self.addMenu(\"&View\")\n\n        # Focus Mode toggle\n        self._focus_mode_action = QAction(\"Toggle Focus Mode\", self, checkable=True)\n        self._focus_mode_action.setShortcut(\"Ctrl+Shift+F\")\n        self._focus_mode_action.setToolTip(\"Enable Focus Mode to reduce distractions\")\n        self._focus_mode_action.triggered.connect(_SafeSlot(self._toggle_focus_mode))\n        view_menu.addAction(self._focus_mode_action)\n        view_menu.addSeparator()\n\n        theme_menu = view_menu.addMenu(\"Theme\")\n        self._theme_menu = theme_menu  # used later for dynamic population\n\n// Add new method after line 368:\n\n    def _toggle_focus_mode(self) -> None:\n        \"\"\"Toggle Focus Mode via the command registry.\"\"\"\n        try:\n            from flockdesk.core.shortcuts.commands import registry\n            registry.trigger(\"core.toggle_focus_mode\")\n            # Update checkbox state based on new setting\n            # The actual state comes from SettingsService\n        except Exception as exc:\n            _logger.error(\"Failed to toggle focus mode: %s\", exc)\n```\n\n### 5. Modify Chat Module ViewModel (chat_vm.py)\n\n**File: `/app/project/flockdesk/modules/chat/viewmodel/chat_vm.py`**\n\nAdd Focus Mode subscription and state management:\n\n```python\n// File: /app/project/flockdesk/modules/chat/viewmodel/chat_vm.py\n// Lines to add after line 94 (in Qt Signals section):\n\n    # Focus Mode\n    focusModeChanged = QtCore.Signal(bool)  # is_snoozed\n\n// Lines to add after line 111:\n\n        # Focus Mode state\n        self._is_snoozed: bool = False\n\n// Lines to modify in _register_event_subscriptions() (add after line 232):\n\n        # Focus Mode changes\n        self._event_bus.subscribe(\n            \"focus.mode.changed\",\n            self._on_focus_mode_changed,\n            group=f\"vm_{self._channel_id}\",\n        )\n\n// Add new property after line 123:\n\n    @property\n    def is_snoozed(self) -> bool:\n        \"\"\"Return whether chat notifications are snoozed due to Focus Mode.\"\"\"\n        return self._is_snoozed\n\n// Add new handler method after line 337:\n\n    # -----------------------------------------------------------------------#\n    # Focus Mode handler\n    # -----------------------------------------------------------------------#\n    def _on_focus_mode_changed(self, event: Event) -> None:\n        \"\"\"Handle Focus Mode state changes.\"\"\"\n        payload = event.payload\n        enabled = payload.get(\"enabled\", False)\n\n        self._is_snoozed = enabled\n        self.focusModeChanged.emit(self._is_snoozed)\n\n        if enabled:\n            _log.info(\"Chat notifications snoozed (Focus Mode enabled)\")\n        else:\n            _log.info(\"Chat notifications resumed (Focus Mode disabled)\")\n\n// Modify _on_incoming_message to suppress notifications in Focus Mode:\n// Lines to modify in _on_incoming_message() after line 258:\n\n        self._append_message(msg)\n\n        # Suppress notification if Focus Mode is active\n        if not self._is_snoozed:\n            # Trigger notification (existing notification logic would go here)\n            pass\n```\n\n### 6. Modify Chat Module View (chat_widget.py)\n\n**File: `/app/project/flockdesk/modules/chat/view/chat_widget.py`**\n\nAdd visual snooze indicator:\n\n```python\n// File: /app/project/flockdesk/modules/chat/view/chat_widget.py\n// Lines to add in _setup_ui() after line 144:\n\n        # Snooze indicator for Focus Mode\n        self._snooze_indicator = QtWidgets.QLabel(\"Notifications snoozed\")\n        self._snooze_indicator.setObjectName(\"snoozeIndicator\")\n        self._snooze_indicator.setStyleSheet(\"\"\"\n            #snoozeIndicator {\n                background-color: #FFA500;\n                color: white;\n                padding: 4px 8px;\n                border-radius: 4px;\n                font-size: 12px;\n            }\n        \"\"\")\n        self._snooze_indicator.setVisible(False)\n\n// Lines to modify in main_layout construction (around line 153):\n\n        main_layout = QtWidgets.QVBoxLayout(self)\n        main_layout.addWidget(self._snooze_indicator)  # Add snooze indicator at top\n        main_layout.addWidget(self._list_view, 1)\n        main_layout.addWidget(self._plugin_container, 0)\n        main_layout.addLayout(bottom_bar)\n\n// Lines to add in _connect_signals() after line 169:\n\n        # Focus Mode indicator binding\n        self._vm.focusModeChanged.connect(self._on_focus_mode_changed)\n\n// Add new slot method after line 240:\n\n    @QtCore.Slot(bool)\n    def _on_focus_mode_changed(self, is_snoozed: bool) -> None:\n        \"\"\"Update snooze indicator visibility based on Focus Mode state.\"\"\"\n        self._snooze_indicator.setVisible(is_snoozed)\n```\n\n### 7. Modify Dashboard Module View (dashboard_widget.py)\n\n**File: `/app/project/flockdesk/modules/dashboard/view/dashboard_widget.py`**\n\nAdd visual de-emphasis for activity feed:\n\n```python\n// File: /app/project/flockdesk/modules/dashboard/view/dashboard_widget.py\n// Lines to add in DashboardWidget.__init__() after line 256:\n\n        self._vm.focusModeChanged.connect(self._on_focus_mode_changed)\n\n// Add new slot method after line 421:\n\n    @Slot(bool)\n    def _on_focus_mode_changed(self, is_deemphasized: bool) -> None:\n        \"\"\"\n        Apply visual de-emphasis to the dashboard when Focus Mode is active.\n        Reduces opacity of the activity feed to minimize visual distractions.\n        \"\"\"\n        if is_deemphasized:\n            # Apply de-emphasis effect (reduced opacity)\n            effect = QtWidgets.QGraphicsOpacityEffect(self)\n            effect.setOpacity(0.5)\n            self.setGraphicsEffect(effect)\n            self.setStyleSheet(\"\"\"\n                #flockdeskDashboard {\n                    background-color: rgba(128, 128, 128, 0.3);\n                }\n            \"\"\")\n            logger.debug(\"Dashboard de-emphasized (Focus Mode enabled)\")\n        else:\n            # Remove de-emphasis effect\n            self.setGraphicsEffect(None)\n            self.setStyleSheet(\"\")\n            logger.debug(\"Dashboard emphasis restored (Focus Mode disabled)\")\n```\n\n## Summary\n\n- **Primary finding**: The Focus Mode feature requires modifications across 8 files following the existing architectural patterns: EventBus for decoupled communication, SettingsService for persistence, Command pattern for actions, and MVVM for module UI.\n\n- **Key components**:\n  1. `configs/default_settings.json` -
